- **speak-human-agelocked**: AGELOCKED 專屬的繁體中文去 AI 味改寫 skill。用「先保事實、再去 AI 味、最後保住 YiKun 的聲音」的順序，審查與改寫部落格文章、FB／Threads 貼文、電子報、LINE 訊息、產品介紹頁。 它在通用去 AI 味之上，內建 AGELOCKED 的三件事：①保護清單（DOI 引用、價格、產品名、CTA、醫療法規語言、「也就是說」白話翻譯裝置）②YiKun 的 23 點寫作聲音（要保留，不是要洗掉）③這個文章庫實測出來的兩大 AI 味慣性（破折號過密、否定平行結構浮濫）。 觸發時機：「這篇去 AI 味」「幫我校對再發」「這段好 AI」「潤一下」「改自然一點」，或撰稿完要交稿前的自查。 不要觸發：逐字翻譯、事實查核（非風格問題）、frontmatter／程式碼、要求「重寫成別人的語氣」。去 AI 味 ≠ 加人設，這個 skill 只把套路刮掉、把 YiKun 留下。 ([jockersung/agelocked-blog](https://github.com/jockersung/agelocked-blog/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/speak-human-agelocked))

- **speaking-style**: 模仿特定人物說話／用詞習慣的語感規格書集合，目前收錄竹子（Willy）。當需要用某個人的語感產出文字（部落格文章、社群貼文、留言、回覆、簡報講稿、自我介紹、MR 說明、對外訊息），或需要檢查一段文字「像不像那個人講的話」並改寫時使用。也用在「這段讀起來很 AI」要去掉 AI 腔的時候。人物語感放在 personas/ 底下，各自含高頻詞庫、程度副詞與形容詞庫、反問語庫、主題關鍵字庫、專有名詞與中英夾雜慣例、地區用語硬規則、減少 AI 文體的規則、寫完自檢清單。不觸發：程式碼撰寫、commit message、技術文件（規格書／知識庫）等制式產出。 ([willy874/willy874.github.io](https://github.com/willy874/willy874.github.io/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/speaking-style))
- **speaking-the-users-language**: Use when naming anything a person reads — UI copy, field labels, enum values, API field names, CLI flags, error text, table columns, config keys — or when the term in the code differs from the term the business uses. Symptoms: raw enum values on screen, labels named after columns, ISO timestamps, cents shown as cents, implementation words like null/payload/entity leaking into copy. ([TerminalGravity/usability-heuristics-skills](https://github.com/TerminalGravity/usability-heuristics-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/speaking-the-users-language))
- **speaking-what-the-field-knows-anxiety-audience-and-structure**: Use when preparing a talk or judging speaking advice: what this field actually knows versus what it repeats, speech anxiety and the interventions that genuinely reduce it, audience and purpose analysis, structure and the patterns that hold attention, and openings and closings. Includes the router for the whole speaking, influence and community reference. ([the-vibey-project/vibey](https://github.com/the-vibey-project/vibey/tree/HEAD/src/vibey_tools/skills/plugins/speaking-influence-and-community/skills/speaking-what-the-field-knows-anxiety-audience-and-structure) · ★5)
- **speaknotes-openclaw**: Use when OpenClaw needs to call SpeakNotes API routes directly using an API key and generate transcripts/summaries from YouTube URLs, media files, or document files. - **speakpen**: Read the user's SpeakPen voice notes — ideas they spoke into their phone or browser, transcribed and summarized — and use them as the user's own thinking. Use when the user asks what they said, recorded, noted or were thinking about something ("what did I say about pricing?", "my notes from this week", "the idea I recorded while walking"), wants their spoken ideas summarized, compared, turned into a plan, tasks or a draft, or mentions SpeakPen. Read-only. ([Qumge/speakpen-skill](https://github.com/Qumge/speakpen-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/speakpen))
- **speakr-skill**: Transcribe recordings; fetch transcripts or summaries. ([ikelvingo/speakr-skill](https://github.com/ikelvingo/speakr-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **speakstream-streaming-tts-eval**: Evaluates the quality and latency of streaming text-to-speech models that generate audio incrementally from interleaved text and speech inputs. It probes the model's ability to maintain speech accuracy and naturalness while minimizing first-token latency under streaming constraints. Use when the user wants to benchmark on LJSpeech, LibriSpeech, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Word Error Rate (WER). ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/speakstream-streaming-tts-eval) · ★3)
- **speakturbo-tts**: Give your agent the ability to speak to you real-time. Talk to your Claude! Ultra-fast TTS, text-to-speech, voice synthesis, audio output with ~90ms latency. 8 built-in voices for instant voice responses. For voice cloning, use the speak skill. ([emzod/speak-turbo](https://github.com/emzod/speak-turbo) · 1k installs)
- **speakturbo-tts**: Give your agent the ability to speak to you real-time. Talk to your Claude! Ultra-fast TTS, text-to-speech, voice synthesis, audio output with ~90ms latency. 8 built-in voices for instant voice responses. For voice cloning, use the speak skill. Prevents the strong-start-weak-finish failure mode by forcing an explicit MECE rubric and a Plan→Execute→Assess inner loop until the rubric passes. ([Synter-Media-AI/free-skills](https://github.com/Synter-Media-AI/free-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/spear) · ★5)

- **spearkit**: Write and edit Discord bots built with the spearkit package ("discord.js++"): type-safe slash commands, options, subcommands, autocomplete, buttons / select menus / modals with custom-id routing, events, cooldowns, scheduled tasks, prefix commands, guards, context menus, pagination / confirm, preset embeds, structured logging, usage tracking and dotenv. Use whenever a file imports from "spearkit", or when asked to build or modify a Discord bot, slash command, button, select menu, modal, autocomplete, or interaction handler in a spearkit project. ([byigitt/spearkit](https://github.com/byigitt/spearkit/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/spearkit))
- **spearman-agreement-statistics**: Use when validating that an experimentally derived isotopic ratio metric (e.g. R13C from LC/HRMS peaklists) agrees with theory computed from molecular formulas across a benchmark set of authentic standards. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-agreement-statistics))
- **spearman-correlation**: Measures the rank-order agreement between an NLG evaluator's predicted scores and human reference judgments. It probes the evaluator's ability to capture task-specific quality dimensions such as fluency, coherence, consistency, and groundedness across summarization and dialogue generation. Use when the user has predictions and gold and needs to compute Spearman correlation. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/spearman-correlation) · ★3)
- **spearman-correlation-across-ko-families**: Use when you have paired 16S amplicon and shotgun metagenome samples and need to quantify agreement between predicted (PICRUSt) and directly annotated KO abundance profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/spearman-correlation-across-ko-families))
- **spearman-correlation-agreement-analysis**: Use when two per-pair strain-relatedness scores (e.g., SynTracker APSS and inStrain popANI) have been computed on the same sample pairs and you must test whether the two methods agree, or calibrate a joint same-strain cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/spearman-correlation-agreement-analysis))
- **spearman-correlation-analysis**: Use when you have per-tool significant-ASV counts across many datasets plus a table of dataset characteristics and need to test whether DA output volume tracks effect size, sample size, sequencing depth, or sparsity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/spearman-correlation-analysis))
- **spearman-correlation-analysis**: Use when two processed abundance matrices (e.g., metabolomics and proteomics from the same samples) must be integrated via pairwise Spearman correlations to construct a metabolite-protein network and identify hub nodes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-correlation-analysis--0ea9306f105c9780))
- **spearman-correlation-analysis**: Use when ranking metabolic features (e.g. from 1H NMR metabonomic profiles) against a continuous phenotype such as BMI while adjusting for epidemiological confounders like Age, Gender, or T2D, and when bootstrap confidence intervals are needed to validate the associations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-correlation-analysis--499368cedc547901))
- **spearman-correlation-analysis**: Use when testing whether a gene-expression signature (e.g., aggregate TMG or NMG scores from the 73/186 DEGs) is associated with a continuous drug-response phenotype such as GDSC IC50 across cancer cell lines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-analysis--41b0e7dc69b14605))
- **spearman-correlation-analysis**: Use when validating predicted per-cluster or per-lineage drug sensitivity scores (e.g. AUC_prediction.csv from scDrug --model PRISM) against an observed ordered response such as treated-vs-control cell-proportion changes, without assuming linearity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-analysis--95bc88ceb640a2de))
- **spearman-correlation-analysis-of-peak-areas**: Use when two peak-integration approaches have produced matched per-target peak-area tables (e.g., log2 AUC per target per run) and you must demonstrate their quantitative agreement via rank correlation, regression, and precision (CV) benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-correlation-analysis-of-peak-areas))
- **spearman-correlation-analysis-of-read-counts**: Use when comparing per-feature (e.g. BSJ) read-count quantitation across two sequencing platforms or library preparations and you need a rank-correlation coefficient restricted to features detected above a minimum count threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/spearman-correlation-analysis-of-read-counts))
- **spearman-correlation-background-distribution-analysis**: Use when after cross-validated neural network or regression models have generated predicted metabolite abundances and you need to distinguish genuinely predictable metabolites from those with spuriously high correlations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-background-distribution-analysis))
- **spearman-correlation-benchmark-scoring**: Use when comparing alignment methods (e.g. scTopoGAN vs UnionCom, MMD-MA, SCOT) on their ability to predict one modality from another — ATAC↔RNA on Full PBMC or ADT↔RNA on BM — from unsupervised aligned embeddings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-benchmark-scoring))
- **spearman-correlation-benchmarking**: Use when comparing feature-imputation methods on unpaired multimodal single-cell data where held-out features (e.g. smFISH-measured genes) have ground-truth spatial or quantitative values to correlate against predictions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-benchmarking--2abcc99799f0929d))
- **spearman-correlation-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating how well predicted reaction/metabolite fluxes (e.g., from METAFlux compute_flux on NCI-60 RNA-seq) agree with matched experimental flux measurements, via rank-based Spearman correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-benchmarking--1f9a786f894dfad3))
- **spearman-correlation-cell-similarity**: Use when mapping leukemia/carcinoma sub-cluster cells onto a reference scRNA-seq atlas (e.g., AML1_cluster1 onto normal BMMCs) and a rank-based, outlier-robust expression similarity term is needed as one component of a composite statistic. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-cell-similarity))
- **spearman-correlation-coefficient-calculation-cross-validation**: Use when you have trained predictive models (e.g., MiMeNet neural networks) on microbiome-metabolome paired data using k-fold cross-validation, held out test folds for each fold-iteration, and generated continuous abundance predictions for metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/spearman-correlation-coefficient-calculation-cross-validation) · 2 copies)
- **spearman-correlation-computation**: Use when evaluating a retention-order prediction model (e.g. ROASMI, Morgan_count fingerprints) against experimental elution orders on multiple RPLC datasets, where per-dataset rank agreement must be aggregated into an average rank score. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-correlation-computation))
- **spearman-correlation-computation**: Use when : (1) you have metabolomic data (NMR or MS-derived) and a continuous phenotype variable; (2) you need to quantify associations while controlling for known confounders (age, gender, disease status); ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-computation))
- **spearman-correlation-computation-2d-genomic-predictions**: Use when evaluating 2D contact map predictions (e.g., Hi-C-derived maps from the DNALONGBENCH contact_map_prediction task) for models such as Akita, CNN, HyenaDNA, or Caduceus, where rank-based correlation is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-computation-2d-genomic-predictions))
- **spearman-correlation-computation-microbiome**: Use when you have cross-validated predictions of metabolite abundances from a microbiome-metabolome model and need to: (1) measure predictive accuracy at the individual metabolite level; (2) aggregate performance across all metabolites to report mean SCC; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-computation-microbiome))
- **spearman-correlation-cost-function**: Use when implementing or re-implementing CAPITAL's dynamic-programming tree alignment and you must score candidate matched nodes between two branched trajectory trees by expression similarity rather than nearest-neighbor cell pairing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-cost-function))
- **spearman-correlation-dimension-reduction**: Use when clustering large-scale scRNA-seq data with a subsampling-clustering-classification strategy (e.g. k-means on 20-80% subsampled cells) and you need a dimension-reduced feature space that retains clustering accuracy (NMI/ARI) better than PCA or raw expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-dimension-reduction))
- **spearman-correlation-edge-filtering**: Use when building metabolite-metabolite network edges from measured concentrations or peak intensities (e.g., GC-TOF-MS plasma metabolite data across samples) and biochemical pathway or structural annotations are missing, so empirical co-variation is the only available association signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-correlation-edge-filtering--471564ef7f1882da))
- **spearman-correlation-edge-filtering**: Use when building omics co-occurrence or multi-omics networks from a feature (species/metabolite/gene) abundance table in MetaNet, and you need defensible correlation thresholds instead of an arbitrary similarity cutoff before downstream topology, module, or keystone analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-correlation-edge-filtering--dcee3423de7ffa9f))
- **spearman-correlation-feature-computation**: Use when building the CRIT feature matrix and you need a gene-level circRNA co-expression score from paired mRNA/circRNA FPKM matrices (e.g., MiOncoCirc fpkm_matrix.csv) to quantify gene–circRNA regulatory coupling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-feature-computation))
- **spearman-correlation-feature-projection**: Use when clustering large-scale scRNA-seq data (e.g., tens of thousands of cells) with subsampling, and raw-expression clustering accuracy or 10%-vs-50% subsample consistency is poor; the goal is to cluster in a Spearman-correlation feature space instead. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-feature-projection))
- **spearman-correlation-gene-ranking**: Use when you have OnClass prediction probability matrices (e.g. pred_Y_seen.released.npy, pred_Y_all.released.npy) plus single-cell expression data and need to identify or validate marker genes for seen and unseen Cell Ontology terms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-gene-ranking))
- **spearman-correlation-heatmap-clustering**: Use when several normalization variants (e.g., tGAM, rGAM, rLOESS, QC-RLSC, QC-RSC, SERFF, TIGER) have been applied to the same dataset and you need to visualize how similarly they rank metabolites by differential abundance signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-correlation-heatmap-clustering))
- **spearman-correlation-metric-computation**: Use when scoring predicted 3D genome contact maps from models such as Caduceus-Ph or HyenaDNA against observed maps on the DNALONGBENCH contact map prediction task, or when quantifying agreement (SCC, alongside PCC) for specific genomic regions like chr6:145,205,248-145,614,848. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-metric-computation))
- **spearman-correlation-of-factor-loadings**: Use when Tensor-cell2cell (or similar TCA) factor loadings must be compared against a reference decomposition or a context-level phenotype such as COVID-19 severity, and a rank-based, distribution-free agreement metric is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-of-factor-loadings))
- **spearman-correlation-of-ko-abundance-profiles**: Use when a 16S OTU table with a PICRUSt-predicted metagenome must be validated against a shotgun-annotated KO table (e.g. HUMAnN v0.98 KOs), especially across rarefied sequencing depths to find where prediction accuracy converges or crosses over. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/spearman-correlation-of-ko-abundance-profiles))
- **spearman-correlation-of-marker-expression-with-confidence**: Use when cells carry a nominal annotation over an underlying gradient (e.g. EMT, development) and you need to check that DNN confidence scores track marker gene expression in a monotonic way. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/spearman-correlation-of-marker-expression-with-confidence))
- **spearman-correlation-omics-behavioral-factors**: Use when relating continuous multi-omics factor loadings (e.g., MOFA factors from methylation/metabolomics) to behavioral dimension scores in a cohort where no family structure needs modeling, and a rank-based association measure is preferred. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-correlation-omics-behavioral-factors))
- **spearman-correlation-performance-assessment**: Use when a paired microbiome-metabolome model (e.g. MiMeNet, MelonnPan, NED) has been trained and cross-validated and you need per-metabolite SCC values plus a shuffled-data empirical background to declare which metabolites are well-predicted. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-correlation-performance-assessment))
- **spearman-correlation-prediction-evaluation**: Use when reproducing or benchmarking metabolite abundance prediction from paired microbiome-metabolome count data and per-metabolite SCC values, mean SCC, and well-predicted metabolite counts are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spearman-correlation-prediction-evaluation))

- **spearman-correlation-recovery-evaluation**: Use when comparing denoising performance of scRNA-seq methods against a ground-truth full-depth profile, especially to test whether a method that denoises cells independently outperforms neighbor-aggregating methods on rare cell populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-recovery-evaluation))

- **spearman-correlation-robustness-assessment**: Use when you need to measure how much adding or deleting a percentage of edges in a regulatory or kinase-substrate network changes downstream activity inference, and whether the change is statistically significant. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-robustness-assessment))

- **spearman-correlation-statistical-testing**: Use when comparing regression or classification models for sgRNA activity prediction on datasets like CRISPRoff_tiling (111,682 sgRNAs, 520 genes) and you need rank-based correlation plus significance testing across CV folds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/spearman-correlation-statistical-testing))
- **spearman-correlation-threshold-derivation**: Use when you need an objective, dataset-specific significance threshold for per-metabolite prediction correlations in a paired microbiome-metabolome regression task, rather than an arbitrary fixed SCC cutoff. - **spearman-correlation-trajectory-progression**: Use when you have a reconstructed gene regulatory network (CLR/CellNet-style edge list) plus per-cell trajectory progression values from CellRouter and want to rank regulators driving or mediating specific cell-state transitions. - **spearman-correlation-validation**: Use when a single-cell or ensemble-derived continuous genomic track (e.g., compartment eigenvector E1) must be quantitatively validated against a matching bulk assay track on a common genome assembly and bin grid. - **spearman-correlation-weight-initialization**: Reference for initializing TF weight matrices when fitting TFvelo gene dynamics models, where candidate TFs (ENCODE/ChEA) need expression-correlation-based starting weights per target gene. - **spearman-cross-correlation-lr-tf-covariation**: Use when you have two single-cell trajectories (e.g. EPI and PE lineages from blastoid/blastocyst scRNA-seq ordered by monocle3), a ligand-receptor pair list, and RTN master-regulator results, and need to find co-varying LR–TF pairs prior to Granger causal testing. - **spearman-distance-k-medoids-clustering**: Use when you have simulated (or real) per-feature expression/accessibility profiles across multiple cell types and need to verify that features with shared simulated regulatory programs group into coherent profile clusters. - **spearman-distance-kmedoids-clustering**: Use when validating that a single-cell multi-omics simulation (e.g. sc_mosim scRNA-seq/scATAC-seq output) reproduced the intended cell-type-specific expression profiles, by clustering gene profiles and measuring misassignment rates. - **spearman-fold-difference-correlation-analysis**: Use when validating inferred miRNA activity against measured single-cell or purified-cell-type miRNA profiles, by correlating predicted vs observed fold-differences between cell-type pairs across miRNAs. - **spearman-injection-order-analysis**: Use when a quantified concentration table from a targeted LC-MS/MS sequence (e.g. porcine plasma) must be checked for residual injection-order drift after applying a drift-correction strategy, or when comparing correction strategies for compound-specific residual drift. - **spearman-pearson-correlation-analysis**: Use when comparing read-level coverage tracks of synthetic vs real scRNA-seq/scATAC-seq data binned into genomic windows (e.g., 1000-bp bins on chr1) to quantify simulator fidelity. - **spearman-rank-correlation**: Use when you have per-sample gene-signature scores and a continuous per-sample metadata variable (purity/VAF) in a pdacR dataset and need a rank-based association with rho, p-value, and n. - **spearman-rank-correlation-analysis**: Use when validating Nanodynamo inference on simulated RNA life-cycle data by correlating modelled vs expected RNA species abundances and inferred vs expected synthesis, processing, degradation, export, and translation rates per gene.
